### Spec Compliance

- ✅ Spec compliant. Every item in the brief has a hunk:
  - `GoalCard` (`goal-card.tsx:67-155`): props unchanged, ring 64, gold `ProgressBar` with the same a11y label, `toGoLine` or `reachedTitle`, whole card is the "See it" link, no-goal invitation unchanged, `useGoalMoments` called.
  - `GoalScreen` (`goal-screen.tsx:261-312`):
    - ring 200 with the label `${name}, ${saved}`, name as `text-h1`, "X of Y", `MilestoneStars`, gold chip with the weeks line.
    - `keycap-gold` "Save some" goes to put-in.
    - Reached shows "Ask {parent} to buy it" via `useParentLabel()`, which opens take-out.
    - `GoalSheetButton` stays.
  - `goal-staircase.tsx` is deleted and its barrel line removed.
  - The route title comes from `goalCopy.page.heading` (`app/(child)/goal.tsx`).
  - The diff touches no Home source, `(child)/_layout.tsx` or `(tabs)/**`.
  - Tests listed in the brief are present. The monthly-plan weeks case is extra and fine.
- ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff:
  - `goalCopy.page.heading` is "My goal".
  - The `GoalRing` testID `goal-ring` and its `accessibilityElementsHidden` behaviour. The new card test depends on both, and `goal-ring.tsx` is Task 6.
  - The "never mind"/overshoot path of `toGoLine` returning null. The overshoot test relies on it.
  - Nothing else still imports `goal-staircase`. The brief's `grep -rn GoalStaircase apps/mobile` is outside this diff.

### Strengths
- The `use-goal-moments.ts` rewrite fixes both carried bugs.
  - **Re-run on mid-run change:** `again` is set when a trigger arrives mid-run and the loop re-runs. Props are read from `latest.current` after the storage await (`use-goal-moments.ts` `handle()`, around lines 605-610), and `lit` is recomputed from the fresh view instead of the stale closure.
  - **Await before release:** `await mark.mutateAsync` happens inside the run, so the `runs` entry is held until the mark settles.
  - **No second celebration:** `useMarkCelebrated.onSuccess` (`use-goal-mutations.ts:52`) writes `celebratedAt` into the query cache before `mutateAsync` resolves. A second mounted component therefore sees the settled view, and `reachedDone` covers the window where it still holds the old one.
- Both new tests in `use-goal-moments.test.tsx` target the real races:
  - A held-open mark with two mounts produces one celebration and one mark call.
  - A star lit during a slow storage read still cheers.
- `try/catch` around the mark logs only `error.name`, which matches the public-repo hygiene rule.
- `GoalScreen` passes `null` until the currency is known, so celebrations can't run with an empty currency.
- Test updates follow the brief, and the screen tests assert behaviour, not just mocks.

### Issues

#### Critical (Must Fix)
None.

#### Important (Should Fix)
None.

#### Minor (Nice to Have)
1. **Offline replay (the implementer's flag): acceptable as designed, but not bounded to a new session.** `use-goal-moments.ts` `handle()`, `reachedDone` is local to one run chain. If `mark.mutateAsync` rejects, `celebratedAt` stays null. The effect deps (`goalId, lit, reached`) don't change, so nothing loops. But any later fresh trigger replays the full celebration in the same session: a remount, for example opening the goal page from Home while the card stays mounted underneath. That matches the "shows again next time" comment, and it is one extra replay per remount, not a loop. If you want it capped, keep a module-level `Set` of goal ids already celebrated this session and consult it alongside `reachedDone`.
2. **A stalled mark blocks later cheers.** A `mutateAsync` that never settles keeps the `runs` entry held (`use-goal-moments.ts`, the `runs.delete` in `finally`). It would block star cheers for that goal in the same session. I found no `networkMode` or `onlineManager` setup in `apps/mobile/src`, so an offline call should reject rather than pause, which makes this unlikely. Release `runs` before awaiting the mark, but keep `reachedDone` set first, if you want to remove the risk.
3. **Link accessibility on the card.** `goal-card.tsx:129-133`: the whole `Pressable` is a link labelled only "See it". On a real screen reader this likely collapses the children into one element. The goal name, amount, progressbar and to-go line would then be hidden, even though RNTL's `getByRole("progressbar")` passes. The brief mandates the "See it" label, so consider adding an `accessibilityHint` or a fuller label that includes the goal name and amount.
4. **The `home-screen.test.tsx` mock is justified.** `GoalCard` now mounts `useGoalMoments`, which needs the celebrate provider, and Home source stays untouched. It does mask integration with the provider, which is fine for a Home test. `useParentLabel` isn't mocked there. That works only because the real hook doesn't need extra data. If it starts to, this test will break for an unrelated reason.

### Assessment

**Task quality:** Approved

**Reasoning:** The goal page and card match J2 and the brief, props are unchanged, and the staircase is cleanly removed. The moments fix closes both races with tests that fail on the old code. The offline-replay case you flagged only replays on a fresh mount and is not a loop. The Home test mock is a legitimate consequence of `GoalCard` now mounting `useGoalMoments`.

### Spec Compliance

- ✅ Spec compliant on domain behaviour. Payloads and state machine match the base (528c73f9):
  - Purchase payload is still `{ amount, category: category ?? "", label, idempotencyKey }` through `useRecordPurchase` (purchase-sheet.tsx `submit`). Test purchase-sheet.test.tsx:535-540 asserts the exact outbox call.
  - On the base, `sent` and `queued` fell through to the same `removeItem(replaces)` (base lines 101-103). The new `sent` branch keeps that `removeItem` before `celebrate`. `refused`, `unsaved` and `unsent` still set refused or notice. The `sending` ref guard is kept, and the double-press test still passes (test:809-825).
  - Received payload is unchanged (`{ ...parsed.data, label: parsed.data.label ?? "" }`). The `UNAVAILABLE` → notice / else → refused split is unchanged. The new `return` after catch stops a celebration on failure. Test diary-more.test.tsx:349-359 asserts the payload and :393 asserts no celebration on refusal.
  - Idempotency key is made once on mount and reused on retry (test:681-697). A new opening gets a new key (test:699-717).
  - Undo is kept as the celebration's secondary, gated on `entryId` (purchase-sheet.tsx `celebrate({... secondary: entryId ? ...})`). Received has no Undo, as before.
  - "Mum" comes from `useParentLabel()` (received-sheet.tsx `JarChoice parent={parent}`). `flash`/`SentToast` are gone from both sheets.
  - The moment-card a11y label now includes the chip (moment-card.tsx:153). It is tested at home-screen.test.tsx:890-896. The Home budget is now ≤ 30 and has a moment-card case (home-screen.test.tsx:872-888).
- ⚠️ Cannot verify from diff:
  - `edges={["bottom"]}` on purchase.tsx:16 and received.tsx:10 is reasoned only, not checked on a device. The controller should check it on iOS and Android with the formSheet detent.
  - Brief deviation: there is no `aria-invalid` on the keypad amount. The test asserts the schema message instead (purchase-sheet.test.tsx:559-574). The error is still announced, because amount-keypad.tsx:62-65 uses a polite live region. Acceptable.

### Strengths
- The old/new sheet diff is careful: the fall-through `removeItem` is preserved on `sent`, the `sending` guard is untouched, and `sparks.snapshot()` runs before `mutateAsync` in both sheets.
- `next()` validates step 1 with the same schema as the send, and `submit` jumps back to step 1 on an amount error. A stale amount can't be sent from step 2.
- `JarChoice` signature change is safe: no other callers in src/, app/ or tests/ (grep checked).
- `useSparkChips().chips()` never throws (use-spark-chips.ts try/catch). So the received sheet's blank "under the celebration" view can't be left without a celebration on top. Android back runs `primary` (celebration.tsx `onRequestClose={onPrimary}`), so it closes the sheet.
- Tests check real outbox and RPC payloads and query invalidations, not just render snapshots. The "Next needs an amount" test gets its expected message from `purchaseSchema` rather than hardcoding it.

### Issues

#### Critical (Must Fix)
None.

#### Important (Should Fix)
1. **The keyboard can now cover the name field and the submit button.**
   - Where: purchase-sheet.tsx and received-sheet.tsx (step 2 bodies), flows/flow-shell.tsx:64-81.
   - What changed: the base sheets used `KeyboardScrollView` (`KeyboardAwareScrollView` with `bottomOffset`). The new step 2 runs inside FlowShell, which uses a plain RN `ScrollView` with a fixed footer.
   - Why it matters: the "Name it" `Input` sits below a 3×3 grid in purchase. In received it sits below the grid, two jar picks and the goal line. On iOS, focusing it will likely put the field and the keycap under the keyboard. That is a layout regression from the old sheets.
   - Fix: use `KeyboardScrollView` (or `KeyboardAvoidingView` around the footer) in FlowShell. FlowShell is Task 4's file, so either do it there or note it as a follow-up.
   - Device-verify afterwards.

#### Minor (Nice to Have)
1. **Success haptic fires twice.**
   - `use-record-purchase.ts:46` and `use-record-received.ts:35` already fire `notificationAsync(Success)`. `CelebrationProvider` fires it again (celebration.tsx effect).
   - Now that every success celebrates, either drop the hook haptic in child flows or the provider's.
2. **Undo has no busy state after the celebration closes.**
   - purchase-sheet.tsx saved view: the old busy/disabled Undo button was removed. While `undo.isPending`, the view shows only `entrySavedMessage` and Done, with no progress indicator.
   - Consider an `ActivityIndicator` while `undo.isPending`.
3. **Hardcoded jar names.** received-sheet.tsx: `receivedFlowCopy.submit(jar === "save" ? "Save" : "Spend")` duplicates jar names that already exist as `receivedFlowCopy.save.label` / `spend.label`.
4. **Received borrows purchase copy.** received-sheet.tsx imports `purchaseFlowCopy` for `next`, `celebrate`, `celebrateSub`, `nice` and `nameHint`. That couples the two copy objects. Shared keys in a common flow copy, or re-exported on `receivedFlowCopy`, would read cleaner.
5. **Goal line can say "£0 to go".** received-sheet.tsx `toGo` floors at 0, so a gift that reaches the goal shows "⚽ Boots: £0 to go after this!". This follows the brief literally, but a "reached" wording would be friendlier.
6. **No RED run (process).** The report admits the tests were rewritten after the implementation, so no failing run was captured. That goes against brief Step 2.

### Assessment

**Task quality:** Approved

**Reasoning:** Payloads, outbox and refusal handling, idempotency and undo match the base sheets, and the tests pin each of them. The one real risk is that the keyboard may cover step 2's name field and button since dropping `KeyboardScrollView`. It lives in Task 4's FlowShell and should be fixed or tracked before device QA.

## Final whole-branch review: Plan 5 (feat/mobile-kid-learn, ef1e7ed8..ae925e47)

I reviewed it in one pass. I read the whole-branch diff, the ledger, the plan's constraints, Review Focus and Tasks 7–9, the spec's Learn section, and the source of core quest, option-art and checkin plus the mobile checkin, learning and learn files. I did not run the suite.

**What holds up**
- **Mission player frozen.** The `-U0` grep for the frozen functions (`check`, `send`, `continueOn`, `busy`, `CurrentStepContext` value and the rest) prints nothing. Accessible names are unchanged, and `learn.test.tsx` changed only the heading strings, two mocks and one new Hint assertion.
- **Layering.** core `learning` → `../progress` and `../mascot`; `progress` → `../mascot` and `../lib`. No cycle. Relative imports follow the `progress/copy.ts` precedent. No purity breach.
- **Mobile imports.** Mobile `learning` imports only components, lib and core. `checkin` → `auth`, `jars` (`useHome`), `progress`, so it cannot form a cycle with Home.
- **Reduced motion.** `Confetti` and `Jarso` gate on `useReducedMotion`. `playSound` respects the sound toggle.
- **Check-in day logic is family-local end to end.** `checkinDecision` uses `familyToday(tz)` and `localDateOf(readAt, tz)`, and the hook takes `tz` from `useHome`. The `askedDay` ref is re-checked inside `decide()`, so two overlapping focus runs can't push twice. A failed reread returns early.

### Critical
None.

### Important (fix before merge)
1. **Check-in screen fails contrast in dark mode.** `apps/mobile/src/features/checkin/checkin-screen.tsx:34`
   - The screen uses `bg-primary`, which is `#6facc2` in dark mode, under `text-white` (heading, streak, rest day) and `text-teal-100` (eyebrow).
   - White on `#6facc2` is about 2.5:1, which fails even the 3:1 large-text bar. `teal-100` on it is worse.
   - Fix: use the theme-stable `bg-teal-700` (it equals light `primary`).
2. **A hint revealed after a wrong answer can land off-screen.** `apps/mobile/src/features/learning/mission-player.tsx:887` and `:900`
   - After a wrong (non-final) answer, the Hint button sits right under the answer sheet, near the top.
   - Tapping it adds the hint to `JarsoHint`, which renders below the picture tiles. On a long step the child taps Hint and sees nothing change.
   - This is the ledger's T6 deferred item; I'm promoting it.
   - Fix: while `outcome && !outcome.final`, render `JarsoHint` (or just the hint list) directly after the sheet and Hint button, or move `JarsoHint` above `StepInput`.
3. **Picture-tile letters are unreadable in dark mode.** `apps/mobile/src/features/learning/picture-options.tsx:201–203`
   - The letter uses `text-grape-700` on `bg-tint-grape`, which is `#2a2550` in dark mode: about 2:1.
   - Most reason and predict options get the letter fallback, so this is the main "picture" on many steps.
   - Fix: `text-grape-700 dark:text-grape-200` (the `dark:` variant is already used in `components/ui/input.tsx`).

### Minor
- **Idle line after a settled step.** `mission-player.tsx:834,900`: `JarsoHint` still shows `coach.says.idle` ("Take your time.") below an answered, settled step. Main hid the line once feedback showed. Hide `JarsoHint` when `outcome?.final`, or keep only the hints.
- **Child's Jarso look missing in lessons.** `answer-sheet.tsx:467` has a `look` prop that nothing passes. `JarsoHint` and the `DoneBadge` Jarso also get no look. The map and check-in show the child's chosen look, but lessons show the default. Either pass `useProgress().data?.look` through, or drop the dead prop.
- **Unused `fill` prop.** `quest-map.tsx:1316`: `QuestMap`'s `fill` is never passed. Drop it.
- **Dead core output.** `packages/core/src/learning/quest.ts`: `jarso.x` is computed (clamped `+0.9*stop`), but the UI ignores it in favour of `jarsoCenterX`. `ChestReward.earned` is also unused. Either delete them or note they are for later use.
- **Barrel exports.** `features/learning/index.ts` now exports `PictureOptions`, `JarsoHint` and `jarsoCenterX`, but only the module itself uses them (and tests could import relatively). `ON_GRAPE = "white"` is duplicated in `next-step-card.tsx` and `quest-map.tsx`.
- **Sparks pill icon in dark mode.** `learn-screen.tsx:364`: `ZapIcon` uses `palette.grape["600"]` on dark `tint-grape`, which is low contrast. The pill's label carries the meaning, so this is cosmetic.
- **Doc mismatch in design.md.** `docs/design.md` says "options with art words are picture tiles", but every choice, predict and reason option is now a tile (letter fallback). Reword it.
- **Doc note in mobile.md.** `docs/mobile.md` lists `checkin` under pages. Task 9 should move it to sheets when it adds it to `SHEETS`.
- **Static halo hidden under reduced motion.** `quest-map.tsx:1397`: the "now" halo isn't animated, so hiding it there removes a static cue for no reason. Harmless.
- **Wrong-answer sheet says "Try again" twice.** The heading is "Close! Try again" and the button is "Try again". The spec mandates the heading, so leave it unless design objects.

### Task 9 safety (nothing blocks it; notes)
- `SHEETS` uses `formSheet` with detent 0.9. `CheckinScreen`'s `SafeAreaView` + `flex-1` matches the purchase route, so registering it is fine.
- Nope calls `router.back()` without a `canGoBack()` fallback; purchase does have one. That's safe while the only entry is a push from Home. Mirror purchase's fallback if a deep link is ever added.
- **First-open collisions.** If plan 3's Home pushes anything else on focus (permission ask, celebration), the two pushes can stack. In T9, make sure `useCheckinPrompt` is the only first-open push, or sequence them.
- **`useHome` location.** `useCheckinPrompt` and `LearnScreen` take `useHome` from `@/features/jars`. Plan step 9.1 already covers re-pointing it if plans 3/4 moved it.

### Ledger deferred-minor triage
| Item | Verdict |
|---|---|
| T1-3 Jarso overlap when x clamp bites | **Resolved** by `jarsoCenterX` (T4) |
| T1-3 chest `earned` true while closed | **Resolved** (UI uses `chest.open`, test covers it); `earned` is now dead, see Minor |
| T1-3 option-art unanchored match | **Resolved** in 106a7f98 |
| T1-3 checkin London test passes for the wrong reason | **Fix now** (1 line): it skips via `lastPromptDay === today`, not via the same-day read. Set `lastPromptDay: null` so the test proves its name. |
| T1-3 quest test shadowing / hard-coded 32; vacuous option-art test | Leave |
| T4 flipped-left Jarso near the neighbouring stop | Leave for the device check. By my arithmetic at width 324, Jarso's bubble overlaps the previous stop's bottom edge by about 12px on days 3–5 (column top = `stop.y-76`, previous stop bottom = `stop.y-64`). It's cosmetic and `pointerEvents="none"`. Nudging `top` down 12px would clear it. |
| T4 stale roadmap mentions in tasks/todo.md and wave-2 spec | Leave (historical docs) |
| T5 feedback below options needs scroll | **Resolved** by T6 (sheet under the prompt) |
| T5 unreachable letter fallback; weak decorative test | Leave |
| T6 sheet top-only radius looks odd inline | **Fix now**: cheap, visible on every answer; use `rounded-[24px]` |
| T6 revealed JarsoHint below options | **Fix before merge**: Important #2 |
| T6 finish-card confetti clipped by overflow-hidden | Leave |
| T6 loose `/red/` regex | Leave (it can only false-fail; tighten to `/\bred-\|destructive/` if touched) |

### Declined to judge
- **Check-in lost offline and not queued.** Ruled in the ledger and plan Review Focus 2.
- **Chest shows existing badges and looks without granting them.** Ruled; no migration allowed.
- **Jarso stands beside the stop, not on it.** Accepted ledger behaviour to avoid covering the play button.
- **The placement quiz (NextStep kind "check-in") and the daily check-in share the word "check-in".** Copy owned by plan 2 and main; out of scope.
- **Hints stay visible after the step is settled.** Same as main's code (only the idle line is new, listed in Minor).
- **Device-only visual checks** (map spacing, tile text wrapping for long reason options). They need a device; the e2e selectors are consistent.

**Verdict:** Ready to merge once Important #1–3 and the two cheap minors (sheet radius, London test) are fixed. None of them blocks Task 9.

## Plan 4 whole-branch review: ef1e7ed8..6adfaf7a (feat/mobile-kid-jars)

I did the review read-only and in two passes: core and data first, then mobile and tests. I ran no tests. I also read the plan, the contract, the global constraints, the ledger, the matching `supabase/migrations`, and plan 3's branch (`feat/mobile-kid-home`) to check how Task 9 would integrate.

### Critical
None.

### Important (fix before merge)

1. **The Sparks and badge chips can claim rewards the server never gave.** `apps/mobile/src/features/goals/use-goal-moments.ts:117-118, 142`
   - The chips are hard-coded `+${SPARKS.goalMilestone}`, `+${SPARKS.goalReached}` and `BADGES.goal_reached.name`.
   - The server sometimes pays 0. `supabase/migrations/20261106120100_progress.sql:6,121` says milestones already passed when a goal is created or its target changes are recorded with 0 Sparks, and goal-reached is capped at once per rolling 7 days.
   - So the plan's own "lowering the target cheers the highest star" case shows "+25" that the child never gets. A second goal reached inside 7 days shows "+100", and shows "Goal Getter" again even though that badge is earned once for life.
   - Fix: build the chips from a before/after `useProgress` delta, the way plan 3's `@jarso/core/home` `sparkChips` does. Show the badge chip only if the badge is new. Otherwise drop the chips.

2. **"Steady!" cheers a week where savings went down.** `packages/core/src/ledger/weekly.ts:77`
   - The `cur === prev` check runs before `cur < 0`. Two weeks of −₹5 in a row therefore read "Same as last week. Steady!".
   - Review Focus 1 requires "You used some savings." for a negative week.
   - Fix: move `if (cur < 0)` above the equality check and add a test for it.
   - Related, minor: when last week was negative, `cur > prev` reports "₹40 more than last week!" while this week's bar shows ₹10. Comparing against `Math.max(prev, 0)` would fix it.

3. **`text-money-m` is not a class in the type scale.** `apps/mobile/src/features/goals/goal-screen.tsx:97`
   - `mobileFontSize` only has `money-xl`, `money-l` and `money`, so "₹340 of ₹450" falls back to body size.
   - `tests/type-scale.test.ts` doesn't catch unknown `text-*` sizes.
   - Fix: use `text-money` (or `text-money-l`).

4. **The jars↔goals barrel cycle should be fixed before merge.**
   - `jar-screen.tsx:16` imports `useGoal` from `@/features/goals`, and `goal-screen.tsx` already imports `useHome` from `@/features/jars`.
   - It's safe today because both sides only use each other at render time, and Babel's `export *` keeps live bindings.
   - But it is the only feature-level cycle in the app (I checked the import graph of every feature). Metro prints "Require cycle" in dev, and the first top-level use would break it.
   - Cheap fix in the spirit of "app composes": `app/(child)/jar/[jar].tsx` calls `useGoal()` and passes `goalTargetMinor` to `JarScreen`. Jars then no longer imports goals.

### Minor
- `apps/mobile/tests/features/jars/weekly-bars.test.tsx:56`: "a negative week is a calm stub" checks `bar-current`, which is the positive £1 bar. The −£30 stub's style is never asserted. Give negative bars a testID and assert on that one.
- `jar-screen.test.tsx`: "spend page uses teal…" never asserts the colour.
- `use-goal-moments.ts:143`: "Nice!" is hard-coded copy and should live in `goalCopy.page`. The same goes for "Try again" and "Loading your jar" in `jar-screen.tsx`, if that is the convention.
- `use-goal.ts:43`: `useSavingPace` counts a *paused* schedule as a pace, so "about N weeks" shows while no allowance is coming. Consider `status === "active"`.
- `goal-screen.tsx`: the ring's label (`name, saved`), the header (name) and the saved text all get read out, so a screen reader says the name and amounts twice.
- `goal-card.tsx:120`: the link label leaves out "£20 of £45", and the inner progressbar and header are flattened into the link on device. That's acceptable, just noted.
- `milestone-stars.tsx`: `ZoomIn` plays on every mount, unlit stars included. A star that lights while the page is open only changes colour. Reduced motion is handled correctly.
- `docs/mobile.md:77` has two errors:
  - It says the jar page shows "the jar's history". It doesn't; it shows the two actions.
  - It says `useGoalMoments` is "mounted by the Home goal card". `GoalScreen` mounts it too.
- On this branch before the rebase, Home renders both `HomeStepCard` (whose `GoalReached` marks the goal on mount) and `GoalCard` (`useGoalMoments`). If the mark lands before the AsyncStorage read, the full celebration is skipped. Plan 3's Home doesn't render `HomeStepCard`, so this disappears after the rebase, as long as Task 9 deletes `HomeStepCard`.

### Checked and fine
- **Money:** integer minor units end to end; `weeksToGoal` math is integer then `ceil`.
- **Week boundaries:** Monday `startOfWeek`, conversion via `localDate(occurredAt, timezone)`, and the Kolkata 00:30 Monday test are all correct. Weeks before the history starts count as 0; empty history gives no NaN.
- **Goal moments:**
  - The stored star count only ever goes up, so money leaving or a target raise never cheers.
  - A new goal id is seeded silently.
  - The module-level `runs` map and the `again` re-run stop concurrent double-fires.
  - `onSuccess` writing `celebratedAt` stops a later mount from celebrating again.
  - The tests cover these cases well.
- **`findLatestSchedule`:** `frequency` is in the column grant (`20261101230000_allowance_hide_auto_charge_from_child.sql`), the only callers are on mobile, and the query cache isn't persisted, so there are no stale shapes.
- **Dark mode:** colours come from `useTokens` or token classes; no raw hex.
- **Copy:** within the word budget; the Spend line is neutral.
- **Task 9 safety:** plan 3 doesn't touch `features/jars` or `features/goals` except `predict-step.tsx`, and its Home no longer imports `JarsCard`, `JarGraph` or `HomeStepCard`. `keys.balance` shares the `home` prefix, so refreshing Home also refreshes the bars. Nothing here blocks Task 9.

### Triage of the ledger's deferred minors
1. **Spend line wording:** closed. The T4-5 ruling replaced it with this-week-only copy.
2. **jars↔goals barrel cycle:** fix before merge (Important 4).
3. **Home-step latch survives a target raise:** drop. It goes away when Task 9 deletes `HomeStepCard`; make sure Task 9 does delete it.
4. **`useSavingPace` duplicates `useAllowance`'s query options:** optional cleanup (a shared `scheduleQuery(userId)` helper). Doesn't block; can be folded into the paused-schedule fix.
5. **A failed mark replays the reached celebration on remount:** accept. It's the intended "shows again next time" behaviour, the same as the web and `HomeStepCard`. Once Important 1 is fixed it no longer shows a false "+100".
6. **A stalled mark blocks later cheers in the session:** accept. After reached there are no more stars for that goal id, and other goals use different keys.

### Declined to judge
- How Spend bars look when most weeks are negative (grey stubs): the plan prescribes it.
- The 90-day fetch window: the plan says to keep it until Task 9.
- `asFrequency` throwing on an unknown frequency: there's a DB check constraint, and the behaviour predates this branch.
- AsyncStorage `jarso.goalStars.*` keys never being cleaned up: negligible size, keyed by UUID.

**Verdict:** Not ready to merge yet. Fix Important 1–4 first (all small and local); none is critical, and nothing makes Task 9 unsafe.

### Spec compliance

✅ Spec compliant on domain behaviour and the brief's file list, with one consistency gap (Notice colour, see Important).

- Same hooks and payloads. Deposit sends `{amountMinor, idempotencyKey}` (put-in-sheet.tsx:86-89 in the diff). Withdrawal sends `reason: parsed.data.reason ?? ""`, as before. Emergency now adds `?? ""`, which is harmless because emergencySchema requires a reason (packages/core/src/requests/schemas.ts:27-35). The key is made once per mount and reused across retries and refusals, and tests cover both cases (sheets.test.tsx "reuses the key…" ×3).
- over_available and over_cap use `depositMessage` / `withdrawalMessage` verbatim. take-out-sheet.tsx `refusal` matches the old `isSent` logic exactly. A refusal sends the child back to step 1 with the Notice (ask-reason-flow.tsx `submit`, `setStep(1)`).
- "Save some" still says the cash goes to the parent: the button reads `I'll give £x to Mum` and the celebration reads "Now give Mum £x" / "Then it's in Save" (tests: "pending celebrates…").
- Each limit shows as one chip. Put in uses `copy.limit` = "Spend has £10", with chips capped at availableSpendMinor via `quickChips`. Take out uses "You can ask up to £15". Ask extra has no limit.
- JarsoGuess appears only with an unreached goal: put_in always, take_out only when `guess` is set. Ask extra has none.
- SentToast is deleted. Running `grep SentToast|flash(` over diary/requests finds only parent-request-items.tsx:72, as expected.
- Tone is gold for deposit and teal otherwise. "Track it" uses `router.replace("/(child)/requests")`.
- goals.test.tsx: the two removed take-out cases are covered by sheets.test.tsx "Jarso's guess shows with a goal and not without", which checks both with and without a goal plus the pick state. predict-step.test.tsx covers the guess outcome copy.
- Kit carry-overs are correct. predict-step.tsx drops `disabled` and guards in onPress (test added). use-spark-chips.ts consumes the snapshot once (test added). AskTracker uses an index key with a justified biome-ignore.
- Deviation accepted: with no amount typed, the button label is "Save some" (put-in-sheet.tsx `label`). It stays enabled so the schema's amount error shows.

⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff: the `askCelebration` copy for deposit `confirmed`/`done` (it lives in core flow-copy, Task 3). The controller should confirm that the auto-confirmed deposit copy still tells the child to hand the cash over.

### Strengths
- Extracting `ask-reason-flow.tsx` removes what would otherwise be two near-identical two-step sheets. The wrappers are thin and only pass in what differs (refusal, limit, guess, schema).
- Refusal handling was ported precisely: the same status set and the same message functions, and only the amount step is reopened.
- `fail()` routes amount errors back to step 1, so a schema error on step 2 is never hidden.
- The tests drive the real keypad and picture grid, and assert the real mutation payloads and keys, not mocks of the UI.

### Issues

#### Critical
None.

#### Important
1. **Refusals render destructive red. Every other child error on these screens uses calm `text-primary`.** Plan-mandated: the brief says "as a `Notice`".
   - `Notice` is `text-destructive` (apps/mobile/src/components/sheet/sheet-parts.tsx:128).
   - It is used for over_available / over_cap / offline in put-in-sheet.tsx (`<Notice message={notice} />`) and ask-reason-flow.tsx (step 1 and step 2 `<Notice>`).
   - Task 7 renders its child-facing alerts in `text-primary` (apps/mobile/src/features/diary/purchase-sheet.tsx:180, :273, :278).
   - The same step-2 screen already shows the reason error in `text-primary` (ask-reason-flow.tsx `reasonError`), so one screen mixes red and blue.
   - This goes against the global "calm blue, never red" rule for the child app.
   - Fix: in these child flows, render the notice as `<Text accessibilityRole="alert" className="text-primary">`, matching Task 7. Alternatively, give `Notice` a `tone` prop and leave parent screens red.

#### Minor
1. **Dead code left behind.** `useChildRules` (apps/mobile/src/features/requests/use-request-mutations.ts:72), `RuleLines` (sheet-parts.tsx:111) and `ReasonInput` (sheet-parts.tsx:79) no longer have any users, which I confirmed with grep across apps/mobile/src. Delete them, along with the `findChildRules` mock in sheets.test.tsx if nothing else needs it.
2. **Stale messages stay on screen** (ask-reason-flow.tsx).
   - The step-2 reason error is not cleared when the child types under Other or picks another picture. Only a resubmit clears it.
   - The refusal Notice on step 1 stays while a new amount is keyed, until Next is pressed.
   - put-in-sheet.tsx has the same refusal-Notice behaviour.
   - Fix: clear `fieldErrors` / `notice` in the `onChange` handlers.
3. **Use savings now needs a picture tap.** The submit button stays disabled until a picture is picked (ask-reason-flow.tsx `disabled={!reasonKey || …}`). Sending with no reason now needs a tap on "Other". The brief allows this, but it is a small UX change from the old sheet, where you could skip the reason.
4. **The Save jar can overstate the result.** `saveAfter` is not clamped while `spendAfter` is (put-in-sheet.tsx `spendAfter`/`saveAfter`). If the child types more than Spend holds, Save shows more than is possible before the refusal arrives. Either clamp `x` to availableSpendMinor for the jar display or leave it as is.
5. **Unchecked cast.** `sent.status as AskStatusOf[typeof kind]` in ask-reason-flow.tsx, because `R extends { status: string }`. Tighten `R` so the status type flows from the result type.
6. **Test-quality nits** (sheets.test.tsx):
   - The PutIn test "shows Jarso's guess with an unreached goal and not without" only checks the "with" case.
   - "approved celebrates Mum said yes" repeats the `approved` row of the `it.each` above it.
   - "rejects a bad amount" asserts `findByText(/amount/i)`, which is loose. Assert the schema's exact message instead.
7. **Process.** The report says the sheet tests were written after the implementation, so there was no RED run for the main Task 8 behaviour. Only the carry-overs had RED→GREEN evidence.

### Assessment

**Task quality:** Needs fixes

**Reasoning:** Domain behaviour is preserved: the same hooks, payloads, idempotency lifecycle and verbatim refusal messages. The shared flow is a good abstraction. Child-facing refusals still render in destructive red, though, unlike Task 7 and the reason error on the same screen. That breaks the calm-colour constraint and should be fixed (one-line class change), and the now-dead `useChildRules`/`RuleLines`/`ReasonInput` should go.

**Finding Verdicts**

1. **Check-in dark-mode contrast** — ADDRESSED. `checkin-screen.tsx:38` now uses `bg-teal-700`, a theme-stable fill. The text on it (`text-white`, `text-teal-100`) is unchanged and readable.
2. **Hint lands off-screen after a wrong answer** — ADDRESSED. `mission-player.tsx` builds one `hintBubble` and renders it after `AnswerSheet` and the Hint button while `outcome` is non-final. Otherwise it renders after `StepInput` (`{outcome && !outcome.final ? null : hintBubble}`). The new learn.test presses Hint after the wrong answer and asserts the hint text appears before the option text in the tree.
   - **Frozen-function check:** the grep you specified printed nothing. The diff in that file only adds the `useProgress` and `look` plumbing and the `hintBubble` placement.
   - **`hintsUsed: 1` question:** the change is because the test now presses Hint before the retry. `tries: 2` and the rest of the payload are unchanged, so no behaviour changed.
3. **Picture-tile letters in dark mode** — ADDRESSED. `picture-options.tsx:~38` adds `dark:text-grape-200`.
4. **Answer sheet rounds all four corners** — ADDRESSED. `answer-sheet.tsx` replaces the top-only inline radii with `rounded-[24px]` on the `overflow-hidden` container. The doc comment is updated to match.
5. **London check-in test passed for the wrong reason** — ADDRESSED. `checkin.test.ts` now sets `lastPromptDay: null`. In `checkin.ts`, the `lastPromptDay === today` early skip no longer applies. The skip now comes from the `checkedInToday` branch, after the same-day `readAt` check. So it genuinely exercises the timezone case, and the Kolkata twin still returns "refresh".
6. **Hide JarsoHint idle line once final** — ADDRESSED. `line={outcome?.final ? null : line}`, and `JarsoHint` now takes `string | null`. It renders nothing when there is no line and no hints, and keeps the hints when `line` is null. Both cases have tests in `jarso-hint.test.tsx`.
7. **Thread the child's Jarso look into lesson Jarsos** — ADDRESSED. `useProgress().data?.look` is passed to `AnswerSheet` and `JarsoHint` in `StepView`, and to `DoneBadge` in `MissionPlayer`. A grep found no `<Jarso ` in `features/learning` without a `look`. `AnswerSheet`'s `look` prop is no longer dead.
8. **QuestMap `fill`, `ON_GRAPE`, barrel exports** — ADDRESSED.
   - The `fill` prop is gone from `QuestMap`, and `learn-screen` never passed it.
   - `ON_GRAPE` now lives only in `on-grape.ts`, imported by `next-step-card` and `quest-map`.
   - The barrel no longer exports `jarso-hint` or `picture-options`. Every import of them is relative, apart from `jarso-hint.test`, which imports the module directly; `quest-map.test` now imports from `quest-map` directly.
9. **docs/design.md wording** — ADDRESSED. The Mission player row now says every choice, predict and reason option is a picture tile.

**New Breakage in the Fix Diff**

- Minor: `StepView` calls `useProgress()` for each step, and `MissionPlayer` calls it too. These share one query, so this costs only a re-render, and tests are reported passing.
- Minor: the test titled "the same instant is still yesterday in London" is a slight misnomer, since London is still the same day. The assertion itself is valid.

Otherwise none.

**Out-of-Scope Observations**

None.

**Verdict**

**Fix round:** All findings addressed, no new Critical/Important breakage. The fix report names the covering tests and their results: jest tests/features (481 pass), vitest checkin (6 pass), tsc and biome clean. I did not re-run them.

timestamp: 2026-11-06T07:08:44.332Z
time: 2026-11-06 07:08
chars: 4115

### Spec Compliance

- ❌ Issues found: one requirement, "cancel behaviour unchanged", is regressed (see Important). Everything else in the brief is implemented.
- ✅ Implemented as briefed:
  - Header "My asks", from `trackerCopy.title` (`request-list.tsx`) and `_layout.tsx` PAGES.
  - Open asks (`pending`, `approved`) are cards with a title from the kind, `AskTracker` fed by `trackerSteps`, and `heldLine` only on a pending deposit.
  - "Cancel this" appears only on `pending`. It uses the same mutation, the same `requestAlreadyMovedOnMessage`, and the same accessible name `Cancel ${what.toLowerCase()} of ${amount}`.
  - Closed asks are compact rows with the status chip. The empty state is "Nothing here yet."
  - `PushPermissionCard({ title })` shows the one line plus Turn on / Not now, with no body. Without `title` it is unchanged, so parent Home is unaffected.
  - The card renders only inside the non-empty branch, with copy "Want to hear when {parent} answers?". No push scheduling was added.
  - The error note uses `Notice calm`, so it is not red.
- ⚠️ Cannot verify from the diff:
  - `AskTracker`'s testIDs, `dayInZone`, and the `@/features/push` barrel exporting `PushPermissionCard` are outside the diff. Task 4 owns the first, and the third is exercised by the test import.
  - Whether `useParentLabel` returns the lowercase form that `trackerSteps` expects. It capitalises internally, so a capitalised label like "Mum" would yield "Give Mum the" either way. The test passes.

### Strengths
- The brief's tests are all present and map one-to-one, including the new push-card test, which checks that the title and body are replaced and both buttons remain.
- Cancel logic is kept intact, including `sendOnce`, the busy/disabled state and the accessible name.
- The `title ? null : …` approach keeps `PushPermissionCard` backward compatible with a minimal diff.
- Open and closed asks are split into small components (`OpenAsk`, `ClosedAsk`), and the push card is gated on `requests.data.length > 0`.

### Issues

#### Critical (Must Fix)
None.

#### Important (Should Fix)
- `request-list.tsx`, `OpenAsk` `onCancel`/`note` state versus the `open`/`closed` split (`request-list.tsx:~79–80` and the render branches). The "Cancelled" and `requestAlreadyMovedOnMessage` notes live in `OpenAsk` state.
  - After a successful cancel, `useCancelRequest` invalidates and refetches. The request becomes `cancelled`, so it moves to the `closed` list and renders as `ClosedAsk`, a different component.
  - `OpenAsk` unmounts and `note` is lost. The "Cancelled" and "already moved on" confirmations vanish almost immediately.
  - The old `RequestRow` kept the same key and component, so the note persisted. This is a regression in the unchanged-cancel-behaviour requirement. The existing tests miss it because the mocked list never changes after the cancel.
  - Fix: hold the note per request id in `RequestList` (or in a ref/state passed to both components) and show it on `ClosedAsk` too. Alternatively, render the transient note from the mutation result above the list. Add a test where `list` returns the closed state on refetch and the note is still visible.

#### Minor (Nice to Have)
- `request-list.tsx`, `whenInZone` JSDoc says `"Today, 4:10 pm" style`, but `dayInZone` yields e.g. "2 Nov" (per the report, no "Today"). Fix the comment or the format.
- `ClosedAsk` drops the reason that the old row showed ("2 Nov · Game"). The brief says to "keep the existing status chip" and doesn't mention the reason, so this is only worth confirming. No test covers it.
- The tracker `approved` withdrawal and emergency flows are covered only through `trackerSteps` tests in Task 3. The request-list tests exercise only a pending deposit. Coverage is adequate but thin.

### Assessment

**Task quality:** Needs fixes

**Reasoning:** The feature is faithful to the brief and well structured. The component split silently drops the cancel confirmation and "already moved on" notes once the refetch reclassifies the ask as closed. That violates the "cancel behaviour unchanged" constraint, and no test covers it.

### Finding Verdicts

1. **Goal moments hard-coded +Sparks / badge chips; remove the chips, keep the celebrations** — ADDRESSED. `use-goal-moments.ts` drops the `BADGES`/`SPARKS` import and all three `chips` blocks (the goal-reached chips and the star-cheer chip). The celebrations still fire. The test now asserts `chips` is undefined for both the star cheer and the goal-reached moment (`use-goal-moments.test.tsx`).

2. **`weekLine`: `cur === prev` ran before `cur < 0`, and "more than last week" compared against a negative `prev`** — ADDRESSED. In `packages/core/src/ledger/weekly.ts:75-81`, `cur < 0` is now checked first. The comparison uses `before = Math.max(prev, 0)`. Tracing the cases:
   - Two negative weeks: `cur < 0`, so "You used some savings."
   - `prev = -500`, `cur = 0`: "Every coin counts. Keep going!"
   - `prev < 0`, `cur > 0`: reports `cur` itself as "more than last week".
   
   The new test "does not cheer a save week that only climbed back to zero" covers the second case. I did not see a test for two negative weeks. That case follows from the code order.

3. **`text-money-m` is not in the mobile type scale; the type-scale test should catch unknown money sizes** — ADDRESSED. `goal-screen.tsx` now uses `text-money`. The remaining sizes in use are `text-money`, `text-money-l` and `text-money-xl`. The new type-scale test "uses only money sizes the scale has" checks every `text-money*` hit against the `money*` keys of `mobileFontSize`. The report says it was verified red with `money-m`. I did not re-run it.

4. **jars↔goals barrel cycle: jars must no longer import goals** — ADDRESSED. `jar-screen.tsx` drops `useGoal` and takes an optional `goalTargetMinor` prop (default null). `app/(child)/jar/[jar].tsx` calls `useGoal()` and passes `goal.data?.goal.targetMinor ?? null`. Grep shows `features/jars` has no import from `@/features/goals`. The remaining import is goals → jars (`useHome`), which is one direction only. Two tests were added: the prop changes the fill, and the route passes the target.

5. **Minors** — ADDRESSED, all five:
   - Negative-bar test: `weekly-bars.tsx` sets testID `bar-negative` on the negative bar. The test now fetches `bar-negative` and asserts it has no `backgroundColor`.
   - `useSavingPace`: `select` is now `schedule?.status === "active"`, so paused and stopped both give null. A paused-schedule test exists for the hook and for the goal screen ("£25 to go"). The query is shared through `scheduleQuery`.
   - "Nice!": moved to `goalCopy.page.nice` and used in `use-goal-moments.ts`.
   - Goal screen no longer reads name and amount twice: the ring label is now `goalCopy.page.ringLabel` ("Goal progress"), and the test asserts it.
   - `docs/mobile.md`: updated to the route-passes-target design and the jar page's one-sentence-plus-actions content. It also says `useGoalMoments` is mounted by the Home card and the goal page, which matches the grep (`goal-card.tsx:23`, `goal-screen.tsx:26`).

The report names the covering tests with counts (vitest weekly 23 pass; jest goals+jars+type-scale 84 pass), and they match the test files in the diff.

### New Breakage in the Fix Diff

None. One Minor behaviour note: in `weekly-bars.tsx` the `bar-negative` testID replaces `bar-current` when the current week is negative. That only affects test lookups. The report says the tests pass.

### Out-of-Scope Observations

None.

### Verdict

**Fix round:** All findings addressed, no new Critical/Important breakage.

4. **jars↔goals barrel cycle: jars must no longer import goals** — ADDRESSED. `jar-screen.tsx` drops `useGoal` and takes an optional `goalTargetMinor` prop (default null). `app/(child)/jar/[jar].tsx` calls `useGoal()` and passes `goal.data?.goal.targetMinor ?? null`. Grep shows `features/jars` has no `@/features/goals` import. The remaining import is goals → jars (`useHome`), one direction only. Two tests were added: the prop changes the jar fill, and the route passes the goal target.

5. **Minors** — ADDRESSED, all five:
   - Negative-bar test: `weekly-bars.tsx` gives the negative bar testID `bar-negative`. The test now fetches `bar-negative` and asserts it has no `backgroundColor`.
   - `useSavingPace`: `select` is now `schedule?.status === "active"`, so paused and stopped both give null. A paused-schedule test exists for the hook and for the goal screen ("£25 to go"). The query is shared through `scheduleQuery`.
   - "Nice!": moved to `goalCopy.page.nice` and used in `use-goal-moments.ts`.
   - Goal screen no longer reads name and amount twice: the ring label is now `goalCopy.page.ringLabel` ("Goal progress"), and the test asserts it.
   - `docs/mobile.md`: updated to the route-passes-target design and the jar page's sentence-plus-actions content. It says `useGoalMoments` is mounted by the Home card and the goal page, which matches the grep (`goal-card.tsx:23`, `goal-screen.tsx:26`).

The report (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-06-mobile-kid-rework-4-jars/final-fix-report.md`) names the covering tests with counts (vitest weekly 23 pass; jest goals+jars+type-scale 84 pass). They match the test files in the diff. I did not re-run any tests.

None. One Minor note: in `weekly-bars.tsx` the `bar-negative` testID replaces `bar-current` when the current week is negative. That only affects test lookups, and the report says the tests pass.

### Out-of-Scope Observations

None.

### Verdict

**Fix round:** All findings addressed, no new Critical/Important breakage.

## Final whole-branch review: Plan 3, feat/mobile-kid-home (ef1e7ed8..c7463594)

I read the 360 KB package diff in three passes: core, then mobile src, then tests. I also checked the live tree for celebrate(), KeyboardScrollView, child RLS on allowance_occurrences, the Maestro flows and stale references. I modified nothing and ran no suite.

### Strengths
- **Money domain is unchanged in all five flows.** The hooks are the same and so are the payloads:
  - purchase sends `{amount, category, label, idempotencyKey}` through the outbox;
  - received sends `{...parsed, label}`;
  - deposit sends `{amountMinor, idempotencyKey}`;
  - withdrawal and emergency send `{amountMinor, reason ?? "", idempotencyKey}`.
- **Idempotency key lifecycle is the same.** There is one key per mount, kept across steps, refusals and retries, and every AddSheet pick goes through `router.replace`, so each opening mounts a fresh key.
- **Outbox paths are preserved.** `queued` still shows the waiting screen with no celebration, and `sent` + `replaces` still calls `removeItem` (it used to happen through a switch fall-through; now it is explicit). `refused`, `unsaved` and `unsent` keep their messages.
- **Refusals are unchanged.** `depositMessage` and `withdrawalMessage` are used verbatim, and `over_*` results don't dispatch or celebrate (tests cover this).
- **Undo still works.** It is now the celebration's secondary button. Done shows busy while the undo runs.
- **Tests are strong.** They cover the key reused after a refusal or failure, the `already_sent` result, and over_cap/over_available.
- **Core stays pure.** The requests↔home cycle is type-only at runtime, `selectMoments` and `trackerSteps` are well tested, and refusal and error text in child flows is the calm `text-primary` everywhere.
- `celebrate()` replaced every `SentToast`/`flash()` in child flows. `Jarso` respects reduced motion.

### Critical
None.

### Important
1. **The Maestro e2e flows are stale, and every child flow will fail on device.**
   - `apps/mobile/e2e/sign-in.yaml:8,39` and `parent-sign-in.yaml:10,15` wait for "Spent something?" to detect Home. That text now lives only on the Diary screen.
   - `e2e/money-loop.yaml:8-65` taps "Spent something?", "Amount" (as a TextInput), "Write it down", "Put some in Save", "Take some out", "Move my money" and "Got money". All of these are gone.
   - `e2e/offline.yaml:6,11` has the same problem.
   - The plan never mentions e2e (that is a gap in the plan itself). Fix: update the flows for the + → tile → keypad → grid path, or add a ledger line deferring this with a tracking note.
2. **The Home word budget can go over 30 words.** The test (`tests/features/home/home-screen.test.tsx:257-273`) only covers zero or one moment card. With all three cards (said-yes, due, waiting), `wordCount` gives about 22 words for the cards, 4 for the header and 10 for the tiles: about 36. Fix: either cap moments at 2 in `packages/core/src/home/moments.ts:9` (`MAX_MOMENTS`), or trim the copy and add a 3-card budget test.
3. **The FlowShell footer can sit under the keyboard.** In `apps/mobile/src/features/flows/flow-shell.tsx:82`, the footer is outside `KeyboardAwareScrollView`. `components/screen/keyboard.tsx` itself notes that Android is edge-to-edge and the window no longer shrinks, so this is likely Android as well as iOS, not just the iOS device check the ledger has.
   - When the "Name it" field or the "Other" reason field is focused, the Done / "Ask Mum" button is probably hidden. The child has to dismiss the keyboard first.
   - Fix: wrap the shell in the project's `KeyboardAvoidingView`, or put the footer in a `KeyboardStickyView`.
   - At minimum, check it on a device before merging.
4. **Asks give two haptics.** `features/requests/use-request-mutations.ts:41` still fires `Haptics.notificationAsync` in `useSendRequest`'s onSuccess. `celebrate()` also fires one (`components/celebrate/celebration.tsx`, current-id effect). T7 removed the hook haptic for purchase and received, but deposit, withdrawal and emergency still double up. Fix: drop the hook haptic and update the haptic assertions in sheets.test.

### Minor
- **Undo can't be retried after it fails.** In `features/diary/purchase-sheet.tsx:220-256`, if the undo returns `not_enough_spend` or errors, the post-celebration view shows the error with only a Done button. On main the Undo button stayed for a retry.
- **Sheet routes use different safe-area edges.** `app/(child)/{put-in,take-out,ask-extra,add}.tsx` use a `SafeAreaView` with all edges, while `purchase` and `received` were switched to `edges={["bottom"]}`. This probably gives an uneven top gap above FlowShell (the ledger's "FlowShell safe-area insets" item).
- **The "N waiting" count includes approved asks.** In `app/(child)/add.tsx:12-14`, approved asks count as "waiting", but those are yeses.
- **The Diary still uses old names.** `features/history/diary-screen.tsx:36-39` keeps "Spent something?" next to the new name "I bought something". The `request-list.tsx` cancel label still uses the old `WHAT` words ("Cancel put in of £5"). Plan 3 doesn't own diary-screen.
- **A raw white colour is used.** `features/home/moment-card.tsx:38` uses `bg-white` behind the check icon, which is not a token and will glare in dark mode. `jar-tile.tsx` uses `bg-teal-600`/`text-white`, which the spec mandates, so that one is fine.
- **Withdrawal reason UX.** The reason is optional for withdrawals, but the button needs a picture pick. Choosing "Other" and typing nothing sends `""` (the test at sheets.test:366 pins this), which is a bit roundabout.
- **Some labels go over 3 words.** Examples: "Done · £4 gift for someone", "I'll give £4 to Mum", "Put it in Save". The mockup copy was accepted by ruling, but this is worth a pass.
- **A blank plus tab is reachable.** `app/(child)/(tabs)/plus.tsx` renders nothing, so a deep link to `/(child)/plus` shows an empty screen. A redirect would fix it cheaply.

### Ledger deferred minors: triage
| Item | Verdict |
|---|---|
| moments localeCompare tie-break | leave |
| requests↔home type-only cycle | leave (it is type-only; later, move `capitalise`/`PICTURE_TINTS` into `lib`) |
| askCelebration default branch = emergency | leave; adding an exhaustive `never` check is a cheap optional fix |
| JarsoGuess selected opacity, spark snapshot not cleared, tracker key collision | already fixed in 9a40254d |
| keypad comment "2.50" | still wrong (the regex gives "2.5", `amount-keypad.tsx:28`); trivial fix, optional |
| unused `columns` prop (`picture-grid.tsx:13`) | trivial; remove or leave |
| FlowShell safe-area insets | fix with the edges item above |
| plus tab label under js-tabs / inline tabBarButton remount | leave; device check |
| hero→moment flash; week key ignores today | leave |
| JarGraph/JarsCard/HomeStepCard unused | leave; plan 4 T9 deletes them |
| **FlowShell footer outside keyboard-aware scroll** | **fix or device-verify before merge** (Important #3) |
| kit 44dp test reads source; received borrows purchaseFlowCopy; hardcoded jar names in submit label | leave |

### Declined to judge
- Spend and Save tiles no longer show the held/reserved notes. The domain is unchanged and the tracker shows the held line, so I treated it as a spec-driven simplification.
- Emergency reasons are now picture labels ("Games") instead of free text. The plan's two-step ruling covers it.
- `CHIP` palette classes in request-list (`bg-gold-100 text-gold-700`) are older than this branch.
- Whether Hermes supports `Intl.formatToParts`/`DateTimeFormat`. It is used the same way elsewhere in the app.
- The docs commit 65ee267a is outside the range I was given.

### Verdict
Ready to merge with fixes. Money-domain behaviour matches main in all five flows. Before merging, fix the stale Maestro flows and the Home word-budget overflow, and either fix the FlowShell keyboard footer or confirm it on a device. The ask double haptic is a cheap consistency fix to include.

Fixed. Two separate leaks, both in test code. Source had no bug.

**Root causes:**
1. React Query's default 5-minute `gcTime` timer outlived the run. `tests/flow-render.tsx` (`renderFlow`) and the `useSparkChips` `renderHook` in `kit.test.tsx` used no-retry clients without the repo's usual `gcTime: Infinity`. The "on success" test in `purchase-sheet.test.tsx` built a client with no mutation `gcTime`. This hung `kit.test.tsx` and `diary-more.test.tsx`.
2. `purchase-sheet.test.tsx` "sends once when the button is pressed twice" ran two `fireEvent.press` calls side by side, which overlaps their `act()` calls. React then skipped the unmount cleanups, so Jarso's blink `setInterval` (`src/components/brand/jarso.tsx`, cleanup is correct) stayed alive. I confirmed this: a single press, or turning off the blink, makes it exit. `--detectOpenHandles` showed nothing for either leak.

**Fix:** added `testQueryClient()` to `tests/flow-render.tsx` (no retry, `gcTime: Infinity` for queries and mutations). `renderFlow` now uses it by default, and so do the kit and purchase-sheet tests. The two presses now run inside one outer `act()`. The test still catches a missing double-tap guard: I removed the guard and it failed with 2 calls.

**Commits** (in `/home/ezio/Desktop/Work/8x/Jarso/.claude/worktrees/home`): `dcab71f2` fix(mobile): never GC test query caches so Jest can exit, and `f36106ae` fix(mobile): hold the double-press test in one act().

**Proof:** `timeout 900 pnpm exec jest --maxWorkers=4` exited by itself with rc=0 in 33 s wall time (Jest reported 32.6 s). 79/79 suites and 690/690 tests passed, with no "did not exit" warning. The mobile typecheck and Biome pass, and no jest process of mine is left running.

**Stacked branches:** `feat/mobile-kid-jars` and `feat/mobile-kid-learn` carry the same `purchase-sheet` and `flow-render` code, so they need these commits when rebased. Two older tests on main, `request-list.test.tsx:220` and `history-list.test.tsx:350`, use the same overlapping double-press. They don't hang today because they don't mount Jarso, so I left them alone.

Fixed and committed as `62aa285a` (fix(mobile): unref React Query timers in tests). Root `pnpm test` now exits with rc 0 on two real runs.

**Root cause:**
- I reproduced the hang with `--runInBand`: 690 tests pass, then "Jest did not exit".
- Running each file alone in band, 13 hung, including files main has too: guide, learn, rules-form, allowance-form, gift-form, news, use-progress, request-list.
- I wrapped the timers to log any still pending at the end. Every one was a React Query 5-minute GC timer (`Query`/`Mutation.scheduleGc`), left by test clients that keep the default `gcTime`.
- With parallel workers these leaks stay hidden. When the turbo run puts mobile in one process (it took ~120 s there), Jest stays open until they fire.

**Fix:** only `apps/mobile/tests/setup.ts` changed. It gives React Query's `timeoutManager` a timer provider whose timers are unref'd. Its timers still fire and fake timers still drive them (the global `setTimeout` is read on each call), but they no longer keep Jest open. This covers all ~40 test clients without editing them, and the stacked branches don't change this file, so it should cherry-pick cleanly. No forceExit.

**Proof:**
- `jest --runInBand` from `apps/mobile`: rc 0 in 125 s, 690/690 passed. The only timers still pending were React Query's (now unref'd) and Supabase auth's auto-refresh intervals.
- `timeout 1200 pnpm test` from the worktree root: rc 0 in 120 s, and mobile ran fresh (690 passed).
- A plain second `pnpm test` was a turbo cache replay (0 s), so it doesn't count. `pnpm test --force` then ran everything again: rc 0 in 142 s, 10/10 tasks, mobile 690 passed.
- No jest processes remain.

**Files touched in all three commits:** `tests/setup.ts` (this one), plus `tests/flow-render.tsx`, `tests/features/flows/kit.test.tsx` and `tests/features/diary/purchase-sheet.test.tsx` from `dcab71f2`/`f36106ae`, all under `apps/mobile`. With the setup change, `dcab71f2` is no longer needed to stop the hang, but it is still correct. `f36106ae` is still needed: it fixes the overlapping `act()` that kept Jarso's blink interval alive.
